```css
.skill .pips { position: absolute; bottom: -1.6vh; display: flex; gap: 0.4vh; }
.skill .pips i { width: 1.2vh; height: 0.8vh; background: #444; border-radius: 0.3vh; }
.skill .pips i.on { background: #ffd24a; }
.skill .learn { position: absolute; top: -2.4vh; right: -1vh; width: 5vh; height: 5vh; border-radius: 50%; background: #e2b04a; color: #1a1200;
  font-size: 3.6vh; font-weight: bold; display: none; align-items: center; justify-content: center; box-shadow: 0 0 1vh #ffd24a; }
.skill.can-learn .learn { display: flex; }
.cancel-zone { right: 14vh; bottom: 38vh; width: 10vh; height: 10vh; background: rgba(160,30,30,0.6); border: 0.3vh solid #ff7a6a; font-size: 2vh; display: none !important; }
.cancel-zone.show { display: flex !important; }

.death-overlay { position: absolute; inset: 0; background: rgba(20,20,20,0.45); display: none; flex-direction: column; align-items: center; justify-content: center; pointer-events: none !important; }
.death-overlay.show { display: flex; }
.death-text { font-size: 4vh; color: #ff8a7a; } .death-timer { font-size: 8vh; font-weight: bold; }

.screen { position: absolute; inset: 0; display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center; justify-content: center; gap: 2.5vh;
  background: radial-gradient(ellipse at center, rgba(10,14,10,0.55), rgba(0,0,0,0.85)); }
.title { font-size: 11vh; font-weight: 900; letter-spacing: 1vh; color: #f3d38a; text-shadow: 0 0 3vh rgba(255,120,40,0.6), 0 0.6vh 0 #5a1a0a; }
.subtitle { font-size: 2.6vh; color: #ccc; }
.btn { padding: 1.6vh 5vh; font-size: 3vh; border-radius: 1.2vh; background: linear-gradient(#b3261e, #6d140f); border: 0.3vh solid #e2b04a; color: #fff; cursor: pointer; }
.btn.secondary { background: linear-gradient(#3a3f4a, #20242c); border-color: #888; }
.btn-row { display: flex; gap: 2vh; }
.diff .btn.selected { outline: 0.5vh solid #fff; }
.result-title { font-size: 10vh; font-weight: 900; }
.result-title.win { color: #7be07a; } .result-title.lose { color: #ff6a5a; }
.result-table { border-collapse: collapse; font-size: 2.4vh; background: rgba(0,0,0,0.5); }
.result-table td, .result-table th { padding: 0.6vh 2vh; border-bottom: 1px solid #333; text-align: center; }
.result-table tr.rad td:first-child { color: #7be07a; } .result-table tr.dire td:first-child { color: #ff7a6a; }
.result-table tr.me { background: rgba(255,255,255,0.08); }

.rotate-hint { position: fixed; inset: 0; background: #000; display: none; align-items: center; justify-content: center; font-size: 5vw; z-index: 99; }
@media (orientation: portrait) { .rotate-hint { display: flex; } }
```

`src/ui/hud.ts`:
```ts
import type { Match } from '../game/match';
import type { Camera } from '../render/camera';
import type { AbilitySlot } from '../sim/core/types';
import { Team } from '../sim/core/types';
import { MAP } from '../sim/data/map';
import { xpToReach } from '../sim/data/xpTable';
import { abilityCooldown, abilityManaCost } from '../sim/systems/abilities';
import { canLearn } from '../sim/systems/progress';
import { heroLook } from '../render/heroVisuals';

export const SKILL_SLOTS: AbilitySlot[] = ['Q', 'W', 'E', 'R'];

export interface SkillButton {
  slot: AbilitySlot;
  root: HTMLDivElement;
  cd: HTMLDivElement;
  cdText: HTMLSpanElement;
  learn: HTMLDivElement;
  pips: HTMLDivElement;
  name: HTMLSpanElement;
}

const fmt = (s: number) => `${String(Math.floor(s / 60)).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(Math.floor(s % 60)).padStart(2, '0')}`;

export class Hud {
  readonly root: HTMLDivElement;
  readonly joyZone: HTMLDivElement;
  readonly joyBase: HTMLDivElement;
  readonly joyKnob: HTMLDivElement;
  readonly attackBtn: HTMLDivElement;
  readonly lastHitBtn: HTMLDivElement;
  readonly pushBtn: HTMLDivElement;
  readonly recallBtn: HTMLDivElement;
  readonly pauseBtn: HTMLDivElement;
  readonly cancelZone: HTMLDivElement;
  readonly skills: SkillButton[] = [];
  private acc = 1;
  private minimap: HTMLCanvasElement;
  private q = <T extends Element>(sel: string) => this.root.querySelector(sel) as T;

  constructor(parent: HTMLElement, private readonly match: Match, private readonly camera: Camera) {
    this.root = document.createElement('div');
    this.root.className = 'hud';
    this.root.innerHTML = `
      <div class="hud-tl">
        <div class="portrait"><span class="portrait-initial"></span><span class="portrait-level">1</span></div>
        <div class="vitals">
          <div class="bar hp"><div class="fill"></div><span class="txt"></span></div>
          <div class="bar mp"><div class="fill"></div><span class="txt"></span></div>
          <div class="bar xp"><div class="fill"></div></div>
        </div>
        <div class="gold"><span class="coin">●</span><span class="gold-val">0</span></div>
      </div>
      <div class="hud-top">
        <div class="score"><span class="k-rad">0</span><span class="clock">00:00</span><span class="k-dire">0</span></div>
        <div class="roster"><div class="side rad"></div><div class="side dire"></div></div>
      </div>
      <div class="hud-tr"><canvas class="minimap" width="110" height="320"></canvas><div class="btn-pause">❚❚</div></div>
      <div class="joy-zone"><div class="joy-base"><div class="joy-knob"></div></div></div>
      <div class="actions">
        <div class="cancel-zone">取消</div>
        <div class="btn-attack">攻击</div>
        <div class="btn-small btn-lasthit">补刀</div>
        <div class="btn-small btn-push">推塔</div>
        <div class="btn-recall">回城</div>
        ${SKILL_SLOTS.map((s) => `<div class="skill" data-slot="${s}"><span class="skill-key">${s}</span><span class="skill-name"></span><div class="cd"></div><span class="cd-text"></span><div class="pips"></div><div class="learn">+</div></div>`).join('')}
      </div>
      <div class="death-overlay"><div class="death-text">你已阵亡</div><div class="death-timer"></div></div>`;
    parent.appendChild(this.root);
    this.joyZone = this.q('.joy-zone');
    this.joyBase = this.q('.joy-base');
    this.joyKnob = this.q('.joy-knob');
    this.attackBtn = this.q('.btn-attack');
    this.lastHitBtn = this.q('.btn-lasthit');
    this.pushBtn = this.q('.btn-push');
    this.recallBtn = this.q('.btn-recall');
    this.pauseBtn = this.q('.btn-pause');
    this.cancelZone = this.q('.cancel-zone');
    this.minimap = this.q('.minimap');
    for (const slot of SKILL_SLOTS) {
      const root = this.q<HTMLDivElement>(`.skill[data-slot="${slot}"]`);
      this.skills.push({
        slot, root, cd: root.querySelector('.cd')!, cdText: root.querySelector('.cd-text')!, learn: root.querySelector('.learn')!,
        pips: root.querySelector('.pips')!, name: root.querySelector('.skill-name')!,
      });
    }
    this.buildRoster();
    const me = match.world.getUnit(match.playerUnitId);
    if (me) {
      const look = heroLook(me.defId);
      const p = this.q<HTMLDivElement>('.portrait');
      p.style.background = look.body;
      this.q<HTMLSpanElement>('.portrait-initial').textContent = look.initial;
      for (const b of this.skills) {
        const ab = me.ability(b.slot);
        b.name.textContent = ab ? ab.def.name.slice(0, 2) : '';
        if (ab?.def.targetType === 'passive') b.root.classList.add('passive');
        b.pips.innerHTML = ab ? Array.from({ length: ab.def.maxLevel }, () => '<i></i>').join('') : '';
      }
    }
  }

  private buildRoster(): void {
    const w = this.match.world;
    for (const team of [Team.Radiant, Team.Dire]) {
      const side = this.q<HTMLDivElement>(team === Team.Radiant ? '.roster .rad' : '.roster .dire');
      side.innerHTML = w
        .heroes(team)
        .map((h) => {
          const look = heroLook(h.defId);
          const me = h.id === this.match.playerUnitId ? ' me' : '';
          return `<div class="mini ${team === Team.Radiant ? 'rad' : 'dire'}${me}" data-id="${h.id}" style="background:${look.body}">${look.initial}<div class="dead"></div></div>`;
        })
        .join('');
    }
  }

  update(dt: number): void {
    this.acc += dt;
    if (this.acc < 1 / 15) return;
    this.acc = 0;
    const w = this.match.world;
    this.q<HTMLSpanElement>('.clock').textContent = fmt(w.time);
    this.q<HTMLSpanElement>('.k-rad').textContent = String(w.teams[Team.Radiant].kills);
    this.q<HTMLSpanElement>('.k-dire').textContent = String(w.teams[Team.Dire].kills);
    for (const el of this.root.querySelectorAll<HTMLDivElement>('.mini')) {
      const h = w.getUnit(Number(el.dataset.id));
      if (!h?.hero) continue;
      el.classList.toggle('is-dead', !h.alive);
      (el.querySelector('.dead') as HTMLDivElement).textContent = h.alive ? '' : String(Math.ceil(h.hero.respawnTimer));
    }
    this.drawMinimap();
    const me = w.getUnit(this.match.playerUnitId);
    if (!me?.hero) return;
    const h = me.hero;
    const e = w.balance.economy;
    this.q<HTMLSpanElement>('.portrait-level').textContent = String(h.level);
    const setBar = (sel: string, pct: number, txt?: string) => {
      const bar = this.q<HTMLDivElement>(sel);
      (bar.querySelector('.fill') as HTMLDivElement).style.width = `${Math.max(0, Math.min(1, pct)) * 100}%`;
      const t = bar.querySelector('.txt');
      if (t && txt !== undefined) t.textContent = txt;
    };
    setBar('.bar.hp', me.hp / me.stats.maxHp, `${Math.ceil(me.hp)} / ${Math.round(me.stats.maxHp)}`);
    setBar('.bar.mp', me.mana / Math.max(1, me.stats.maxMana), `${Math.floor(me.mana)} / ${Math.round(me.stats.maxMana)}`);
    const cur = xpToReach(h.level, e.xpTableMult);
    const next = xpToReach(h.level + 1, e.xpTableMult);
    setBar('.bar.xp', h.level >= e.levelCap ? 1 : (h.xp - cur) / Math.max(1, next - cur));
    this.q<HTMLSpanElement>('.gold-val').textContent = String(Math.floor(h.gold));
    for (const b of this.skills) {
      const ab = me.ability(b.slot);
      if (!ab) continue;
      const cd = ab.cooldown;
      const total = abilityCooldown(ab);
      b.cd.style.setProperty('--cd', ab.level > 0 && cd > 0 && total > 0 ? String(cd / total) : '0');
      b.cdText.textContent = ab.level > 0 && cd > 0 ? (cd >= 1 ? String(Math.ceil(cd)) : cd.toFixed(1)) : '';
      b.root.classList.toggle('unlearned', ab.level === 0);
      b.root.classList.toggle('no-mana', ab.level > 0 && ab.def.targetType !== 'passive' && me.mana < abilityManaCost(ab));
      b.root.classList.toggle('can-learn', h.skillPoints > 0 && canLearn(h.level, ab));
      b.pips.querySelectorAll('i').forEach((pip, i) => pip.classList.toggle('on', i < ab.level));
    }
    const death = this.q<HTMLDivElement>('.death-overlay');
    death.classList.toggle('show', !me.alive);
    if (!me.alive) this.q<HTMLDivElement>('.death-timer').textContent = String(Math.ceil(h.respawnTimer));
  }

  private drawMinimap(): void {
    const c = this.minimap;
    const g = c.getContext('2d')!;
    const W = c.width, H = c.height;
    const sx = W / MAP.width, sy = H / MAP.height;
    g.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);
    g.fillStyle = '#1b2a1c';
    g.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
    g.fillStyle = '#6b5a3e';
    g.fillRect((MAP.laneX - MAP.laneHalfWidth) * sx, 0, MAP.laneHalfWidth * 2 * sx, H);
    g.fillStyle = '#2a6f86';
    g.fillRect(0, (MAP.riverY - MAP.riverHalf) * sy, W, MAP.riverHalf * 2 * sy);
    const w = this.match.world;
    for (const u of w.units) {
      if (u.removed) continue;
      const x = u.pos.x * sx, y = u.pos.y * sy;
      const col = u.team === Team.Radiant ? '#5fe05a' : '#ff5a4a';
      if (u.kind === 'building') {
        if (u.building?.type === 'fountain') continue;
        g.fillStyle = u.alive ? col : '#555';
        const s = u.building?.type === 'ancient' ? 12 : 8;
        g.fillRect(x - s / 2, y - s / 2, s, s);
      } else if (u.kind === 'hero') {
        if (!u.alive) continue;
        g.fillStyle = col;
        g.beginPath();
        g.arc(x, y, 5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        g.fill();
        if (u.id === this.match.playerUnitId) {
          g.strokeStyle = '#fff';
          g.lineWidth = 2;
          g.stroke();
        }
      } else if (u.alive) {
        g.fillStyle = col;
        g.fillRect(x - 1.5, y - 1.5, 3, 3);
      }
    }
    const cam = this.camera;
    g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.7)';
    g.lineWidth = 1;
    g.strokeRect((cam.x - cam.worldW / 2) * sx, (cam.y - cam.worldH / 2) * sy, cam.worldW * sx, cam.worldH * sy);
  }

  destroy(): void {
    this.root.remove();
  }
}
```

`src/input/controls.ts`:
```ts
import type { Command } from '../sim/commands';
import type { Match } from '../game/match';
import type { Camera } from '../render/camera';
import type { AimIndicator } from '../render/renderer';
import type { Hud } from '../ui/hud';
import type { Vec2 } from '../sim/core/vec2';
import { dist } from '../sim/core/vec2';
import type { AbilitySlot } from '../sim/core/types';
import type { CastTarget } from '../sim/heroes/types';
import { abilityCastRange, isValidUnitTarget, levelValue } from '../sim/systems/abilities';
import { nearestOf } from '../sim/query';
import { dirChanged, dragToAim, joystickVector } from './aimMath';

const DRAG_THRESHOLD = 18;
const AIM_MAX_DRAG = 120;

interface AimState {
  slot: AbilitySlot;
  pointerId: number;
  center: Vec2;
  current: Vec2;
  dragging: boolean;
}

export class Controls {
  aim: AimIndicator | null = null;
  private queue: Command[] = [];
  private joyPointer: number | null = null;
  private joyOrigin: Vec2 = { x: 0, y: 0 };
  private joyDir: Vec2 | null = null;
  private keyDir: Vec2 | null = null;
  private aimState: AimState | null = null;
  private keys = new Set<string>();
  private mouse: Vec2 | null = null;
  private attackHeldUntil = 0;
  private attackHeld = false;
  private offs: (() => void)[] = [];

  constructor(private readonly hud: Hud, private readonly match: Match, private readonly camera: Camera, canvas: HTMLCanvasElement) {
    this.bindJoystick();
    this.bindButtons();
    this.bindSkills();
    this.bindKeyboard(canvas);
  }

  private on<K extends keyof HTMLElementEventMap>(el: HTMLElement | Window, type: K, fn: (e: HTMLElementEventMap[K]) => void): void {
    el.addEventListener(type, fn as EventListener);
    this.offs.push(() => el.removeEventListener(type, fn as EventListener));
  }

  private player() {
    return this.match.world.getUnit(this.match.playerUnitId);
  }

  private push(c: Command): void {
    this.queue.push(c);
  }

  drain(): Command[] {
    if (this.attackHeld && this.match.world.time >= this.attackHeldUntil) {
      this.push({ type: 'attack', mode: 'smart' });
      this.attackHeldUntil = this.match.world.time + 0.5;
    }
    const q = this.queue;
    this.queue = [];
    return q;
  }

  destroy(): void {
    for (const f of this.offs) f();
    this.offs = [];
  }

  // ---------- 摇杆 ----------
  private bindJoystick(): void {
    const zone = this.hud.joyZone;
    const base = this.hud.joyBase;
    const radius = () => base.getBoundingClientRect().width / 2 || 60;
    const setKnob = (x: number, y: number) => {
      this.hud.joyKnob.style.transform = `translate(${x}px, ${y}px)`;
    };
    this.on(zone, 'pointerdown', (e) => {
      if (this.joyPointer !== null) return;
      this.joyPointer = e.pointerId;
      zone.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
      const r = zone.getBoundingClientRect();
      this.joyOrigin = { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY };
      base.style.left = `${e.clientX - r.left}px`;
      base.style.top = `${e.clientY - r.top}px`;
      setKnob(0, 0);
    });
    this.on(zone, 'pointermove', (e) => {
      if (e.pointerId !== this.joyPointer) return;
      const v = joystickVector(e.clientX - this.joyOrigin.x, e.clientY - this.joyOrigin.y, radius());
      setKnob(v.knob.x, v.knob.y);
      if (dirChanged(this.joyDir, v.dir, 0.08)) {
        this.joyDir = v.dir;
        this.push({ type: 'move', dir: v.dir });
      }
    });
    const end = (e: PointerEvent) => {
      if (e.pointerId !== this.joyPointer) return;
      this.joyPointer = null;
      this.joyDir = null;
      setKnob(0, 0);
      this.push({ type: 'move', dir: null });
    };
    this.on(zone, 'pointerup', end);
    this.on(zone, 'pointercancel', end);
  }

  // ---------- 普攻 / 补刀 / 推塔 / 回城 ----------
  private bindButtons(): void {
    this.on(this.hud.attackBtn, 'pointerdown', (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      this.attackHeld = true;
      this.attackHeldUntil = 0;
    });
    const release = () => {
      this.attackHeld = false;
    };
    this.on(this.hud.attackBtn, 'pointerup', release);
    this.on(this.hud.attackBtn, 'pointercancel', release);
    this.on(this.hud.attackBtn, 'pointerleave', release);
    this.on(this.hud.lastHitBtn, 'pointerdown', () => this.push({ type: 'attack', mode: 'lastHit' }));
    this.on(this.hud.pushBtn, 'pointerdown', () => this.push({ type: 'attack', mode: 'building' }));
    this.on(this.hud.recallBtn, 'pointerdown', () => this.push({ type: 'recall' }));
  }

  // ---------- 技能键：轻点智能施法，拖动瞄准 ----------
  private bindSkills(): void {
    for (const b of this.hud.skills) {
      this.on(b.learn, 'pointerdown', (e) => {
        e.stopPropagation();
        this.push({ type: 'learn', slot: b.slot });
      });
      this.on(b.root, 'pointerdown', (e) => {
        if (this.aimState) return;
        const ab = this.player()?.ability(b.slot);
        if (!ab || ab.level === 0 || ab.def.targetType === 'passive') return;
        if (ab.def.targetType === 'toggle') {
          this.push({ type: 'toggle', slot: b.slot });
          return;
        }
        b.root.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
        const r = b.root.getBoundingClientRect();
        this.aimState = { slot: b.slot, pointerId: e.pointerId, center: { x: r.left + r.width / 2, y: r.top + r.height / 2 }, current: { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY }, dragging: false };
      });
      this.on(b.root, 'pointermove', (e) => {
        const st = this.aimState;
        if (!st || st.pointerId !== e.pointerId) return;
        st.current = { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY };
        if (!st.dragging && dist(st.current, st.center) > DRAG_THRESHOLD) {
          st.dragging = true;
          this.hud.cancelZone.classList.add('show');
        }
        if (st.dragging) this.aim = this.computeAim(st).indicator;
      });
      const finish = (e: PointerEvent, cancelled: boolean) => {
        const st = this.aimState;
        if (!st || st.pointerId !== e.pointerId) return;
        this.aimState = null;
        this.aim = null;
        this.hud.cancelZone.classList.remove('show');
        if (cancelled || this.overCancel(st.current)) return;
        if (!st.dragging) {
          this.push({ type: 'cast', slot: st.slot });
          return;
        }
        const { target } = this.computeAim(st);
        this.push({ type: 'cast', slot: st.slot, target });
      };
      this.on(b.root, 'pointerup', (e) => finish(e, false));
      this.on(b.root, 'pointercancel', (e) => finish(e, true));
    }
  }

  private overCancel(p: Vec2): boolean {
    const r = this.hud.cancelZone.getBoundingClientRect();
    return r.width > 0 && p.x >= r.left && p.x <= r.right && p.y >= r.top && p.y <= r.bottom;
  }

  private computeAim(st: AimState): { indicator: AimIndicator; target?: CastTarget } {
    const u = this.player()!;
    const ab = u.ability(st.slot)!;
    const range = abilityCastRange(u, ab);
    const { dir: d, ratio } = dragToAim(st.current.x - st.center.x, st.current.y - st.center.y, AIM_MAX_DRAG);
    const dir = d ?? { x: Math.cos(u.facing), y: Math.sin(u.facing) };
    const cancel = this.overCancel(st.current);
    const origin = { x: u.pos.x, y: u.pos.y };
    const radiusVal = levelValue(ab.def.values.radius, Math.max(1, ab.level));
    switch (ab.def.targetType) {
      case 'direction': {
        const len = range || levelValue(ab.def.values.distance, Math.max(1, ab.level)) || 900;
        return { indicator: { kind: 'direction', origin, range: len, dir, width: levelValue(ab.def.values.width, 1) || 120, cancel }, target: { dir } };
      }
      case 'point': {
        const p = { x: u.pos.x + dir.x * range * ratio, y: u.pos.y + dir.y * range * ratio };
        return { indicator: { kind: 'point', origin, range, point: p, radius: radiusVal || 150, cancel }, target: { point: p } };
      }
      case 'unit': {
        const p = { x: u.pos.x + dir.x * range * ratio, y: u.pos.y + dir.y * range * ratio };
        const cands = this.match.world.units.filter((t) => isValidUnitTarget(u, ab, t) && dist(t.pos, u.pos) <= range + 300);
        const t = nearestOf(p, cands);
        return { indicator: { kind: 'unit', origin, range, targetId: t?.id, cancel }, target: t ? { unitId: t.id } : undefined };
      }
      default:
        return { indicator: { kind: 'none', origin, range: radiusVal || 300, cancel } };
    }
  }

  // ---------- 键盘鼠标（网页调试） ----------
  private bindKeyboard(canvas: HTMLCanvasElement): void {
    const slotKeys: Record<string, AbilitySlot> = { KeyQ: 'Q', KeyW: 'W', KeyE: 'E', KeyR: 'R' };
    const moveKeys = ['ArrowUp', 'ArrowDown', 'ArrowLeft', 'ArrowRight', 'KeyA', 'KeyD', 'KeyS'];
    const updateKeyDir = () => {
      let x = 0, y = 0;
      if (this.keys.has('ArrowUp')) y -= 1;
      if (this.keys.has('ArrowDown') || this.keys.has('KeyS')) y += 1;
      if (this.keys.has('ArrowLeft') || this.keys.has('KeyA')) x -= 1;
      if (this.keys.has('ArrowRight') || this.keys.has('KeyD')) x += 1;
      const dir = x || y ? { x: x / Math.hypot(x, y), y: y / Math.hypot(x, y) } : null;
      if (dirChanged(this.keyDir, dir, 0.01)) {
        this.keyDir = dir;
        this.push({ type: 'move', dir });
      }
    };
    this.on(window, 'keydown', (e) => {
      if (e.repeat) return;
      const code = e.code;
      if (moveKeys.includes(code)) {
        this.keys.add(code);
        updateKeyDir();
        return;
      }
      const slot = slotKeys[code];
      if (slot) {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (e.shiftKey) this.push({ type: 'learn', slot });
        else this.castAtMouse(slot);
        return;
      }
      if (code === 'Space') {
        e.preventDefault();
        this.push({ type: 'attack', mode: 'smart' });
      } else if (code === 'KeyT') this.push({ type: 'recall' });
    });
    this.on(window, 'keyup', (e) => {
      if (moveKeys.includes(e.code)) {
        this.keys.delete(e.code);
        updateKeyDir();
      }
    });
    this.on(canvas, 'mousemove', (e) => {
      this.mouse = { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY };
    });
    this.on(canvas, 'mouseleave', () => {
      this.mouse = null;
    });
  }

  private castAtMouse(slot: AbilitySlot): void {
    const u = this.player();
    const ab = u?.ability(slot);
    if (!u || !ab || ab.level === 0) return;
    if (ab.def.targetType === 'toggle') {
      this.push({ type: 'toggle', slot });
      return;
    }
    if (!this.mouse) {
      this.push({ type: 'cast', slot });
      return;
    }
    const p = this.camera.screenToWorld(this.mouse);
    if (ab.def.targetType === 'unit') {
      const cands = this.match.world.units.filter((t) => isValidUnitTarget(u, ab, t) && dist(t.pos, p) <= 300);
      const t = nearestOf(p, cands);
      this.push(t ? { type: 'cast', slot, target: { unitId: t.id } } : { type: 'cast', slot });
      return;
    }
    this.push({ type: 'cast', slot, target: { point: p } });
  }
}
```

`src/ui/screens.ts`:
```ts
import type { Match } from '../game/match';
import type { Difficulty } from '../sim/core/types';
import { Team } from '../sim/core/types';
import { netWorth } from '../sim/systems/progress';
import { DIFFICULTY_NAMES } from '../ai/difficulty';

const el = (parent: HTMLElement, html: string): HTMLDivElement => {
  const d = document.createElement('div');
  d.className = 'screen';
  d.innerHTML = html;
  parent.appendChild(d);
  return d;
};

export function showStartScreen(parent: HTMLElement, onStart: (d: Difficulty) => void): HTMLDivElement {
  let diff: Difficulty = 'normal';
  const s = el(parent, `
    <div class="title">中路风云</div>
    <div class="subtitle">3v3 · 单路对决 · 先推倒对方遗迹者获胜</div>
    <div class="btn-row diff">
      ${(['easy', 'normal', 'hard'] as Difficulty[]).map((d) => `<div class="btn secondary${d === 'normal' ? ' selected' : ''}" data-d="${d}">${DIFFICULTY_NAMES[d]}</div>`).join('')}
    </div>
    <div class="btn start">开始游戏（斧王）</div>
    <div class="subtitle">左侧摇杆移动 · 右侧技能 · 拖动技能键瞄准</div>`);
  s.querySelectorAll<HTMLDivElement>('.diff .btn').forEach((b) =>
    b.addEventListener('click', () => {
      diff = b.dataset.d as Difficulty;
      s.querySelectorAll('.diff .btn').forEach((x) => x.classList.toggle('selected', x === b));
    }),
  );
  s.querySelector('.start')!.addEventListener('click', () => {
    s.remove();
    onStart(diff);
  });
  return s;
}

export function showPauseMenu(parent: HTMLElement, onResume: () => void, onQuit: () => void): HTMLDivElement {
  const s = el(parent, `<div class="title" style="font-size:8vh">已暂停</div><div class="btn-row"><div class="btn resume">继续</div><div class="btn secondary quit">退出对局</div></div>`);
  s.querySelector('.resume')!.addEventListener('click', () => { s.remove(); onResume(); });
  s.querySelector('.quit')!.addEventListener('click', () => { s.remove(); onQuit(); });
  return s;
}

export function showResultScreen(parent: HTMLElement, match: Match, onRestart: () => void, onMenu: () => void): HTMLDivElement {
  const w = match.world;
  const me = w.getUnit(match.playerUnitId);
  const win = me ? w.winner === me.team : w.winner === Team.Radiant;
  const mins = `${Math.floor(w.time / 60)}分${Math.floor(w.time % 60)}秒`;
  const rows = w
    .heroes()
    .map((h) => {
      const s = h.hero!;
      const cls = `${h.team === Team.Radiant ? 'rad' : 'dire'}${h.id === match.playerUnitId ? ' me' : ''}`;
      return `<tr class="${cls}"><td>${h.team === Team.Radiant ? '天辉' : '夜魇'} · ${h.name}${h.id === match.playerUnitId ? '（你）' : ''}</td><td>${s.level}</td><td>${s.kills}/${s.deaths}/${s.assists}</td><td>${s.lastHits}</td><td>${Math.round(netWorth(h))}</td></tr>`;
    })
    .join('');
  const s = el(parent, `
    <div class="result-title ${win ? 'win' : 'lose'}">${win ? '胜利' : '失败'}</div>
    <div class="subtitle">对局时长 ${mins}</div>
    <table class="result-table"><tr><th>英雄</th><th>等级</th><th>击杀/死亡/助攻</th><th>补刀</th><th>净资产</th></tr>${rows}</table>
    <div class="btn-row"><div class="btn again">再来一局</div><div class="btn secondary menu">返回主菜单</div></div>`);
  s.querySelector('.again')!.addEventListener('click', () => { s.remove(); onRestart(); });
  s.querySelector('.menu')!.addEventListener('click', () => { s.remove(); onMenu(); });
  return s;
}
```

`src/main.ts`:
```ts
import './ui/styles.css';
import { Match } from './game/match';
import { Renderer } from './render/renderer';
import { Hud } from './ui/hud';
import { Controls } from './input/controls';
import { showPauseMenu, showResultScreen, showStartScreen } from './ui/screens';
import { DT } from './sim/core/constants';
import type { Difficulty } from './sim/core/types';

const canvas = document.getElementById('game') as HTMLCanvasElement;
const uiRoot = document.getElementById('ui') as HTMLDivElement;
const hint = document.createElement('div');
hint.className = 'rotate-hint';
hint.textContent = '请将手机横屏游玩';
document.body.appendChild(hint);

const renderer = new Renderer(canvas);

interface Session { match: Match; hud: Hud | null; controls: Controls | null; followId: number | null; demo: boolean; frozen: boolean }
let session: Session | null = null;
let paused = false;
let resultShown = false;
let lastDifficulty: Difficulty = 'normal';
let acc = 0;
let last = performance.now();

function teardown(): void {
  session?.controls?.destroy();
  session?.hud?.destroy();
  session = null;
}

function startDemo(): void {
  teardown();
  const match = new Match({ seed: Date.now() & 0x7fffffff, radiantHeroes: ['axe', 'axe', 'axe'], direHeroes: ['axe', 'axe', 'axe'], playerSlot: null, difficulty: 'normal', recordEvents: true });
  const followId = match.world.heroes()[0]?.id ?? null;
  session = { match, hud: null, controls: null, followId, demo: true, frozen: false };
  renderer.snapTo(match.world, followId);
  showStartScreen(uiRoot, (d) => startGame(d));
}

function startGame(difficulty: Difficulty): void {
  teardown();
  lastDifficulty = difficulty;
  uiRoot.innerHTML = '';
  const match = new Match({ seed: Date.now() & 0x7fffffff, radiantHeroes: ['axe', 'axe', 'axe'], direHeroes: ['axe', 'axe', 'axe'], playerSlot: 0, difficulty, recordEvents: true });
  const hud = new Hud(uiRoot, match, renderer.camera);
  const controls = new Controls(hud, match, renderer.camera, canvas);
  hud.pauseBtn.addEventListener('click', pause);
  session = { match, hud, controls, followId: match.playerUnitId, demo: false, frozen: false };
  resultShown = false;
  paused = false;
  renderer.snapTo(match.world, match.playerUnitId);
  acc = 0;
}

function pause(): void {
  if (!session || session.demo || paused) return;
  paused = true;
  showPauseMenu(uiRoot, () => { paused = false; last = performance.now(); }, () => { paused = false; uiRoot.innerHTML = ''; startDemo(); });
}

window.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => { if (e.code === 'Escape') pause(); });

function frame(now: number): void {
  const dt = Math.min(0.25, (now - last) / 1000);
  last = now;
  const s = session;
  if (s && !paused && !s.frozen) {
    acc += dt;
    while (acc >= DT) {
      s.match.step(s.controls?.drain() ?? []);
      acc -= DT;
      if (s.match.over) break;
    }
    if (s.demo && s.match.over) startDemo();
    renderer.fx.consume(s.match.world.events.drain(), s.match.world, s.followId, renderer.camera);
    s.hud?.update(dt);
    if (!s.demo && s.match.over && !resultShown) {
      resultShown = true;
      window.setTimeout(() => {
        teardown();
        showResultScreen(uiRoot, s.match, () => startGame(lastDifficulty), () => startDemo());
        session = { ...s, hud: null, controls: null, demo: true, frozen: true };
      }, 1800);
    }
  }
  renderer.render(session?.match.world ?? null, Math.min(1, acc / DT), session?.followId ?? null, paused ? 0 : dt, session?.controls?.aim ?? null);
  requestAnimationFrame(frame);
}

window.addEventListener('resize', () => renderer.resize());
document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => {
  if (document.hidden) pause();
  last = performance.now();
});

startDemo();
requestAnimationFrame(frame);
```

注意：结算后 `session.frozen = true`，画面停在对局结束的那一刻作为结算界面的背景，不再推进，也不会自动开始新的演示局；点"返回主菜单"时才调用 `startDemo()`。

- [x] **Step 4: 运行测试和构建**

Run: `npm test && npm run typecheck && npx vite build`
Expected: 全部通过，`dist/` 构建成功。

- [x] **Step 5: Commit**

```bash
git add -A && git commit -m "feat(ui): joystick & skill controls, HUD, minimap, screens and main loop"
```

- **留给后续阶段：** 其余 9 名英雄、天赋选择界面、神杖和魔晶（P2）；物品与商店（P3）；三层 AI、精英怪、选人、难度对精英怪的影响（P4）；调参（P5）；音效、打包（P6）。
- **类型一致性：** `ResolvedTarget`/`CastTarget`/`AbilityInstance` 定义在 `heroes/types.ts`；`Order.cast` 使用 `AbilityInstance` + `ResolvedTarget`；`drawBars(ctx, u, x, y, isPlayer, viewerTeam)` 在 renderer 中按此签名调用；`Controls` 构造参数 `(hud, match, camera, canvas)` 与 `main.ts` 一致。
